# MS human review — proposed class and case decisions

6 class items; 179 case items. All are unsigned proposals. No sealed pass or adjudication was changed.

Class decisions settle only the named policy facet; concrete stamps, boxes, targets and scope calls remain cases. Every decisive visual mark on an included filing is its own visual_mark case (owner decision 14), bundled by SOS system-number chain, with a region render outside git at `.census-passes/MS/review-renders/<item_id>.png`. Latest append-only label-chain heads are compared once. Reference wording is retained in pairs.wording_diffs.

## Counts by trigger

| Trigger | Class | Case |
|---|---:|---:|
| disagreement | 1 | 3 |
| lifecycle_ambiguity | 0 | 6 |
| date_ambiguity | 4 | 2 |
| visual_mark | 0 | 155 |
| missing_source | 0 | 1 |
| identity | 0 | 6 |
| scope | 1 | 6 |

## Class questions

### C01. Status of a 25-day comment close whose anchor the form leaves unstated?

`ms-policy-a4807e70b6142048c679fdd163cd0b5d14693989c598b1f2499291bd0f45e7d8`; 146 instances; 292 label versions.

**Proposed:** status unknown, precision relative, local_value null; keep the FILED stamp as the signed accounting proxy in relative_anchor/ambiguity and stamp + 25 days only as a non-asserted annotation (signed MS Q2). Same uncomputed-close principle as CT C11 (pending owner signature).

The form states the length (“twenty-five (25) day public comment period”) but no anchor; “supported” would assert a close the source does not fix. A says unknown, B says supported; both leave local_value null. The request close, whose anchor is stated, is supported in both passes.

Owner may accept, reject or specify exceptions by immutable record ID; nothing is applied.

### C02. Keep an explicit opening for the hearing-request window?

`ms-policy-b13a3486bbc7f27980d96c267b1154a3cc0f58f102836161223911f37d8d59d9`; 138 instances; 276 label versions.

**Proposed:** Keep the explicit open role. The request wording states its anchor (“within twenty (20) days after the filing of this notice”), so record relative/supported with null local_value and the FILED stamp as proxy; comment-window openings stay unknown/null. Same as CT C15 (pending owner signature).

A records relative/supported, B unknown/unknown, for every request window. Both passes already keep comment-window openings unknown; no opening instant is computed.

Owner may accept, reject or specify exceptions by immutable record ID; nothing is applied.

### C04. Which relative-close carry-in windows were active at 2026-05-18?

`ms-policy-d4045c4f725e2a5203ad0d6d036ece19101f7fd4ce8005037233f93a34c873bc`; 75 instances; 150 label versions.

**Proposed:** Account by the signed FILED-stamp proxy: a comment/request window is carry_in when stamp + N days (filing day excluded) falls on or after 2026-05-18; otherwise reject it as outside_interval and keep it as historical-support evidence. The date stays a non-asserted annotation, never local_value. Scheduled hearings use their literal dates. Pre-start closures (28578) are cases.

UNIVERSE carry-in rule plus signed MS Q2; the brief keeps relative boundaries as review cases, so both passes left all of them provisional. Each stamp is verified in its own visual item; boundary offsets are listed so the owner can see what a different counting rule would flip.

Owner may accept, reject or specify exceptions by immutable record ID; nothing is applied.

### C05. Does the visually read FILED stamp govern where the bulletin index date differs?

`ms-policy-a0a3b87045a038d8bfb6535a8a5d807500732ccdfe211c7fa22115c2657e5e2b`; 76 instances; 218 label versions.

**Proposed:** Yes. The stamp governs interval and cohort accounting (signed MS Q14; brief); keep the index date as conflicting navigation evidence. Each stamp is still checked in its visual item; mismatches that change scope or cohort remain cases.

Both passes flag the same 76 forms (sign-off decision 2c sends every mismatch to step 5). Most are 1–4 days, stamp later, with no band change.

Owner may accept, reject or specify exceptions by immutable record ID; nothing is applied.

### C06. Reconcile completion counts with final source-view attribution?

`ms-policy-ea8e131f9df1219f64e126974438059b9527b59add884d621cd07250a07de74e`; 10 instances; 54 label versions.

**Proposed:** Final source-view attribution, corrected append-only; same as CT C16 (pending owner signature). Never sum overlapping views or treat a zero slice count as an uninspected source.

Both passes logged the bulletin slices at 0 before body inspection (both self-report it); a few form/cross-check rows differ from direct evidence links. Counts are a diagnostic; the brief permits overlapping source-view attribution.

Owner may accept, reject or specify exceptions by immutable record ID; nothing is applied.
